Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The generation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 133. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 299 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on generation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 133. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 299. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any generation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 567. The projective resolution has length at most 996 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 567 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the generation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 996. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 212.4 (Invariance Principle)
